## Slimes
*Ooze · Classic Fantasy · Trope: The cute first enemy (Dragon Quest) and the evolving hero (Tensura)*

### Blue Slime `F`
**Core** · Charming · Water (weak: Lightning) · 0.4 m · Build: `blob` · Territorial

The Blue Slime is a tiny creature with a dome-shaped gelatinous body, about 0.4 m tall. Its gel is smooth and clean, colored in deep blues with a glassy shine. Survivors describe a teardrop body with a glossy highlight and two dot eyes. It has two bright dot eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It hops in little bursts, surrounded by fine mist and droplets that orbit it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Lightning. Attacks only if you come close to its home; back away to avoid a fight.
- **Drops:** Slime Jelly, Gel Core, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Fanghold +25 more
- **Design:** rig `blob` + parts [drip_trail, crown_slot] · material `translucent_gel_sss` · palette #2f7fd1 #9ad7ff #123a5c · VFX `mist_droplets`

### Green Slime `F`
**Core** · Charming · Nature (weak: Fire) · 0.4 m · Build: `blob` · Swarm

The Green Slime is a tiny creature with a wobbling mound of translucent gel, about 0.4 m tall. Its gel is smooth and clean, colored in leaf greens over bark brown. Its most striking feature is fallen leaves suspended inside its jelly. It has two bright dot eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It waddles, surrounded by drifting pollen and tiny blossoms.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Dangerous in numbers. Area attacks and chokepoints help.
- **Drops:** Slime Jelly, Gel Core, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Fanghold +25 more
- **Design:** rig `blob` + parts [inner_core, face_decal] · material `translucent_gel_sss` · palette #4caf50 #a5d66f #3e2723 · VFX `pollen_petals`

### Red Slime `E`
**Core** · Wild · Fire (weak: Water) · 0.5 m · Build: `blob` · Territorial

The Red Slime is a small creature with a wobbling mound of translucent gel, about 0.5 m tall. Its gel is weathered and natural, colored in ember reds fading to soot black. Guild sketches always show a faint ember core pulsing like a heartbeat. It has dark, wary eyes. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It prowls, surrounded by heat-shimmer and drifting sparks.

- **Field note:** Weak to Water. Attacks only if you come close to its home; back away to avoid a fight.
- **Drops:** Slime Jelly, Gel Core, E-Rank Core

### Poison Slime `E`
**Core** · Menacing · Poison (weak: Light) · 0.6 m · Build: `blob` · Territorial

The Poison Slime is a small creature with a dome-shaped gelatinous body, about 0.6 m tall. Its gel is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in bruise purple with sickly green. ### Mimic Slime `D`
**Core** · Dread · None (weak: Fire) · 1 m · Build: `blob` · Passive

The Mimic Slime is a small creature with a dome-shaped gelatinous body, about 1 m tall. Its gel is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in muted greys and dusty browns. Guild sketches always show a surface that ripples into the shape of whatever it last ate. It has eyes set slightly too far apart. A distinctive marking runs along its back. It glides without moving its legs. It has no visible aura; you hear its breathing before you see it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Leaves you alone unless attacked.
- **Drops:** Slime Jelly, Gel Core, D-Rank Core

## Goblinoids
*Humanoid · Dark Fantasy · Trope: Goblins as horror in numbers (Goblin Slayer); hobgoblin → champion → king evolution*

### Goblin `F`
**Core** · Menacing · None (weak: Fire) · 1 m · Build: `small_biped` · Pack

The Goblin is a small creature with a small, compact humanoid body, about 1 m tall. Its skin is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in muted greys and dusty browns. Guild sketches always show a grin of needle teeth under a stolen tin helmet. It has narrow glowing eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It charges without hesitation. It has no visible aura; you hear its breathing before you see it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Never alone. Kill the leader first and the rest may scatter.
- **Drops:** Goblin Ear, Crude Blade, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm, Fanghold +29 more
- **Design:** rig `small_biped` + parts [nose_hooked, bone_trinkets, weapon_socket, ears_long] · material `rough_skin_leather` · palette #8a8f98 #5b5f66 #c9c2b5 · VFX `dust_puffs`

Its skin is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in arterial crimson and dried-blood brown. Guild sketches always show a grin of needle teeth under a stolen tin helmet. It has eyes set slightly too far apart. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It freezes whenever it is looked at, surrounded by a faint red mist that smells of iron. Red veins of Vesper light crawl under its skin, its eyes glow crimson, and it grows bolder under the red moon.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Goblin Ear, Crude Blade, E-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm, Fanghold +29 more
- **Design:** rig `small_biped` + parts [bone_trinkets, loincloth, ears_long] · material `rough_skin_leather` · palette #8b0000 #d7263d #1a0505 · VFX `blood_mist`

## Kobolds
*Humanoid · Dungeon Crawl · Trope: Dog-headed first-floor monsters (DanMachi), trap-builders (classic tabletop)*

### Kobold Scavenger `F`
**Core** · Menacing · None (weak: Fire) · 0.9 m · Build: `small_biped` · Skittish

The Kobold Scavenger is a small creature with a hunched, wiry humanoid frame, about 0.9 m tall. Its mangy fur is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in muted greys and dusty browns. Its most striking feature is a dog-like head with a chipped snout and a sack of stolen tools. It has burning slit pupils. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It charges without hesitation. It has no visible aura; you hear its breathing before you see it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Flees when hurt. Snares or ranged attacks work best.
- **Drops:** Kobold Fang, Scrap Metal, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm, Iron Hold +23 more
- **Design:** rig `small_biped` + parts [crude_armor, weapon_socket, loincloth, nose_hooked] · material `fur_short` · palette #8a8f98 #5b5f66 #c9c2b5 · VFX `dust_puffs`

### Kobold Miner `E`
**Core** · Wild · Earth (weak: Wind) · 0.9 m · Build: `small_biped` · Ambush

The Kobold Miner is a small creature with a hunched, wiry humanoid frame, about 0.9 m tall. Its mangy fur is weathered and natural, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Guild sketches always show a candle-helmet and a pick worn smooth from use. It has amber predator eyes. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It lopes in long strides, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Wind. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Kobold Fang, Scrap Metal, E-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm, Iron Hold +23 more

## Beasts
*Beast · Classic Fantasy · Trope: The horned rabbit "rookie killer" and wolf packs of every isekai starter forest*

### Horned Rabbit `F`
**Core** · Wild · None (weak: Fire) · 0.4 m · Build: `quad_small` · Territorial

The Horned Rabbit is a tiny creature with a low, springy quadruped build, about 0.4 m long. Its coat is weathered and natural, colored in muted greys and dusty browns. Its most striking feature is a single spiral horn and oversized hind feet. It has alert animal eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It stalks low to the ground. It has no visible aura; you hear its breathing before you see it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Attacks only if you come close to its home; back away to avoid a fight.
- **Drops:** Pelt, Fang, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Bastion, Gearholm +22 more
- **Design:** rig `quad_small` + parts [ears_tall, horn_single] · material `fur_groom` · palette #8a8f98 #5b5f66 #c9c2b5 · VFX `dust_puffs`

## Lycanthropes
*Cursed Beast · Gothic Horror · Trope: Moon-cursed transformation horror*

### Werewolf `D`
**Core** · Dread · Dark (weak: Light) · 2.2 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Ambush

The Werewolf is a person-sized creature with an upright humanoid build, about 2.2 m tall. Its fur is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in ink black with bruised violet. Look for a wolf's head on a twisted human body, torn clothing still clinging. It has eyes that never blink. A distinctive marking runs along its back. It glides without moving its legs, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Cursed Fur, Silver-Bane Claw, D-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm +18 more

### Crag Werebear `C+`

The Crag Werebear is a large creature with a broad-shouldered humanoid build, about 3 m tall. Its fur is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Survivors describe tribal tattoos showing through thick bear fur. It has eyes that catch torchlight like coins. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. **Variant (elemental)** · Dread · Light (weak: Dark) · 2.8 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Ambush

The Gilded Beast is a large creature with a broad-shouldered humanoid build, about 2.8 m tall. Its fur is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in warm gold and pearl white. Its most striking feature is fur that ripples like oil under moonlight. It has eyes that never blink. Faint runes glow along its body when it fights. The Radiant Blood Vesper Alpha is a large creature with an upright humanoid build, about 3.2 m tall. ### Elder Skinchanger `A+`
**Variant (elder)** · Horror · Dark (weak: Light) · 3.8 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Aggressive

The Elder Skinchanger is a large creature with a lean humanoid frame, about 3.8 m tall. Its fur is stretched, stitched and constantly shifting, colored in ink black with bruised violet. Survivors describe half its body stuck mid-transformation, forever. It has no eyes at all, only a wide and patient mouth. An elegant crest and glowing veins of mana mark it as a champion of its kind. It copies the walk of whatever it hunts, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it. It is far larger than its kin, with a battle-scarred silhouette and a commanding presence; others of its kind follow it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Cursed Fur, Silver-Bane Claw, A-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm +18 more

### Ancient Fenrir-Blooded Lord `S`
**Variant (ancient)** · Menacing · Ice (weak: Fire) · 5.4 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Aggressive

The Ancient Fenrir-Blooded Lord is a huge creature with a lean humanoid frame, about 5.4 m tall. Its fur is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in pale blues and frost-white. Look for a crown of frost spikes growing from its mane. It has narrow glowing eyes. Its regal silhouette is crowned by a halo-like ring of aura. It charges without hesitation, surrounded by falling snow motes and frosted ground where it steps. Moss, crystals and centuries-old scars cover its body; it has outlived every hunter sent after it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Cursed Fur, Silver-Bane Claw, S-Rank Core

## Insects & Arachnids
*Insectoid · Classic Fantasy · Trope: Giant insects as low-floor threats; the arachne as an intelligent monster race*

### Giant Ant `F`
**Core** · Wild · Earth (weak: Wind) · 0.6 m · Build: `hexapod` · Swarm

The Giant Ant is a small creature with a six-legged armored body, about 0.6 m long. Its chitin is weathered and natural, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Survivors describe red-brown chitin and mandibles like pliers. It has alert animal eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It stalks low to the ground, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Wind. Dangerous in numbers. Area attacks and chokepoints help.
- **Drops:** Carapace Shard, Silk, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Fanghold, Sylvaran +20 more
- **Design:** rig `hexapod` + parts [wing_cases, carapace_ridges] · material `chitin_gloss` · palette #8b5a2b #c2a878 #4a4a3a · VFX `rock_debris`

### Cave Spider `F+`
**Core** · Menacing · Poison (weak: Light) · 0.7 m · Build: `octopod` · Ambush

The Cave Spider is a small creature with an eight-legged arachnid body, about 0.7 m long. Its chitin is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in bruise purple with sickly green. Survivors describe a pale body that glows faintly in the dark. It has burning slit pupils. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It strides with heavy steps, surrounded by a low toxic haze that clings to the ground.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Carapace Shard, Silk, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Fanghold, Sylvaran +20 more

**Core** · Charming · Light (weak: Dark) · 0.05 m · Build: `swarm` · Swarm

Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It waddles, surrounded by soft radiance and floating motes of light.

### Vesper-Touched Scarab Swarm `S-`
**Variant (corrupted)** · Dread · Dark (weak: Light) · 0.05 m · Build: `swarm` · Ambush

The Vesper-Touched Scarab Swarm is a tiny creature with a cloud of tiny creatures, each one is about 0.05 m long, but the swarm covers several meters. Its chitin is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in ink black with bruised violet. Survivors describe golden beetles that flow like spilled coins. It has eyes that never blink. Its regal silhouette is crowned by a halo-like ring of aura. It freezes whenever it is looked at, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it. Red veins of Vesper light crawl under its skin, its eyes glow crimson, and it grows bolder under the red moon.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.

### Phantom Arachne Matriarch `S+`
**Variant (spirit)** · Horror · Light (weak: Dark) · 8 m · Build: `octopod` · Swarm

The Phantom Arachne Matriarch is a huge creature with a bulbous spider frame on long jointed legs, about 8 m long. Its chitin is stretched, stitched and constantly shifting, colored in warm gold and pearl white. Guild sketches always show a crown of silk and eight eyes like jewels. It has a human-like face in the wrong place. Its regal silhouette is crowned by a halo-like ring of aura. It copies the walk of whatever it hunts, surrounded by soft radiance and floating motes of light. Its body is translucent blue, half in the spirit world; weapons pass through it unless they are blessed or enchanted.

- **Field note:** Weak to Dark. Dangerous in numbers. Area attacks and chokepoints help.
- **Drops:** Carapace Shard, Silk, S-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Fanghold, Sylvaran +20 more
- **Design:** rig `octopod` + parts [web_spinneret, eye_cluster] · material `chitin_gloss` · palette #fff3b0 #ffd166 #ffffff · VFX `holy_motes`

### Meadow Ant `F+`
**Variant (regional)** · Wild · Wind (weak: Fire) · 0.6 m · Build: `hexapod` · Territorial

The Meadow Ant is a small creature with a six-legged armored body, about 0.6 m long. Its chitin is weathered and natural, colored in pale jade and cloud white. Survivors describe red-brown chitin and mandibles like pliers. It has alert animal eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It lopes in long strides, surrounded by swirling leaves and a constant breeze. Adapted to plains lands, it has golden, grass-toned coloring.

## Killer Insects
*Insectoid · Dungeon Crawl · Trope: The rookie-killing ants of the early dungeon floors (DanMachi)*

### Killer Ant `E`
**Core** · Dread · None (weak: Fire) · 1 m · Build: `hexapod` · Swarm

The Killer Ant is a small creature with a low, wide insectoid build, about 1 m long. Its chitin is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in muted greys and dusty browns. Survivors describe a pale soldier ant with a jaw that never stops working. It has pale, pupil-less eyes. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It moves in jerky stop-motion bursts. It has no visible aura; you hear its breathing before you see it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Dangerous in numbers. Area attacks and chokepoints help.
- **Drops:** Killer Mandible, Stinger, E-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Strife Marches, Iron Hold, Sunscar, Xochitl Mire +12 more

It moves in jerky stop-motion bursts, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it. Adapted to cave lands, it has pale, light-starved coloring and oversized eyes.

- **Field note:** Weak to Wind. Dangerous in numbers. Area attacks and chokepoints help.
- **Drops:** Killer Mandible, Stinger, C-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Strife Marches, Iron Hold, Sunscar, Xochitl Mire +12 more

### Vesper-Touched Bone Crawler `E-`
**Variant (corrupted)** · Horror · Dark (weak: Light) · 1.4 m · Build: `aberrant` · Guardian

The Vesper-Touched Bone Crawler is a person-sized creature with an asymmetric body with too many limbs, about 1.4 m tall. Its flesh and bone are stretched, stitched and constantly shifting, colored in ink black with bruised violet. Survivors describe a skeleton reassembled wrong, crawling on its hands. It has no eyes at all, only a wide and patient mouth. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It crawls across ceilings, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it. Red veins of Vesper light crawl under its skin, its eyes glow crimson, and it grows bolder under the red moon.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Protects something nearby: a nest, a treasure, or a place.

Its flesh and bone are patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in deep blues with a glassy shine. Survivors describe a crowned skull with a phylactery chained to its chest. It has pale, pupil-less eyes. An elegant crest and glowing veins of mana mark it as a champion of its kind. It glides without moving its legs, surrounded by fine mist and droplets that orbit it. This Water-attuned variant carries one extra elemental feature, like crystal spikes, in its element's colors.

- **Field note:** Weak to Lightning. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Bone Dust, Grave Cloth, A-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Lirian +16 more
- **Design:** rig `floating` + parts [lantern_core, mask_face] · material `rot_bone` · palette #2f7fd1 #9ad7ff #123a5c · VFX `mist_droplets`

### Elder Vampire Lord `SS`
**Variant (elder)** · Menacing · Blood (weak: Light) · 3.5 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Aggressive

The Elder Vampire Lord is a large creature with a broad-shouldered humanoid build, about 3.5 m tall. Its flesh and bone are rough, scarred and spiked, colored in arterial crimson and dried-blood brown. Guild sketches always show a cape that dissolves into bats. It has eyes that catch torchlight like coins. The air distorts slightly around its outline. It charges without hesitation, surrounded by a faint red mist that smells of iron. It is far larger than its kin, with a battle-scarred silhouette and a commanding presence; others of its kind follow it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Bone Dust, Grave Cloth, SS-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Lirian +16 more
- **Design:** rig `medium_biped` + parts [cloak, horns, weapon_socket, tail] · material `rot_bone` · palette #8b0000 #d7263d #1a0505 · VFX `blood_mist`

### Vesper-Touched Bone Dragon `SS-`
**Variant (corrupted)** · Horror · Dark (weak: Light) · 20 m · Build: `dragon` · Aggressive

The Vesper-Touched Bone Dragon is a colossal creature with a four-legged, two-winged dragon body, about 20 m long. Its flesh and bone are stretched, stitched and constantly shifting, colored in ink black with bruised violet. Survivors describe a dragon skeleton held together by green fire. It has too many eyes, opening one after another. The air distorts slightly around its outline. It unfolds into something larger, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it. Red veins of Vesper light crawl under its skin, its eyes glow crimson, and it grows bolder under the red moon.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Bone Dust, Grave Cloth, SS-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Lirian +16 more
- **Design:** rig `dragon` + parts [spine_plates, horn_crown, tail_blade] · material `rot_bone` · palette #2d2a4a #6b5b95 #0d0d14 · VFX `shadow_wisps`

Survivors describe a stitched sack face with a painted smile that is never in the same field twice. It has no eyes at all, only a wide and patient mouth. It is far larger than its kin, with a battle-scarred silhouette and a commanding presence; others of its kind follow it.

### Phantom Salamander Spirit `D+`
**Variant (spirit)** · Menacing · Light (weak: Dark) · 1 m · Build: `serpent` · Guardian

The Phantom Salamander Spirit is a small creature with a long, sinuous serpentine body, about 1 m long. Its body is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in warm gold and pearl white. Survivors describe a lizard of living flame. It has narrow glowing eyes. A distinctive marking runs along its back. It circles its prey, surrounded by soft radiance and floating motes of light. Its body is translucent blue, half in the spirit world; weapons pass through it unless they are blessed or enchanted.

- **Field note:** Weak to Dark. Protects something nearby: a nest, a treasure, or a place.
- **Drops:** Elemental Essence, Spirit Mote, D-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm, Sylvaran +23 more

### Ancient Undine `C`
**Variant (ancient)** · Charming · Water (weak: Lightning) · 2.4 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Guardian

The Ancient Undine is a person-sized creature with an upright humanoid build, about 2.4 m tall. Its body is smooth and clean, colored in deep blues with a glassy shine. Look for a woman-shaped figure of flowing water. It has wide curious eyes with star-shaped highlights. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. It waddles, surrounded by fine mist and droplets that orbit it. Moss, crystals and centuries-old scars cover its body; it has outlived every hunter sent after it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Lightning. Protects something nearby: a nest, a treasure, or a place.
- **Drops:** Elemental Essence, Spirit Mote, C-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Vharlen, Strife Marches, Gearholm, Sylvaran +23 more
- **Design:** rig `medium_biped` + parts [armor_plates, weapon_socket, mask, horns] · material `elemental_emissive` · palette #2f7fd1 #9ad7ff #123a5c · VFX `mist_droplets`

### Storm Sylph `C-`
**Variant (elemental)** · Charming · Lightning (weak: Earth) · 1.5 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Passive

The Storm Sylph is a person-sized creature with a broad-shouldered humanoid build, about 1.5 m tall. Its body is smooth and clean, colored in storm violet shot through with electric yellow. Survivors describe a translucent dancer of wind and leaves. It has big round glossy eyes. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. It hops in little bursts, surrounded by crackling arcs that jump across its body. This Lightning-attuned variant carries one extra elemental feature, like element-scarred hide, in its element's colors.

- **Field note:** Weak to Earth. Leaves you alone unless attacked.

### Grove Gnome `C-`
**Variant (regional)** · Wild · Nature (weak: Fire) · 0.7 m · Build: `small_biped` · Territorial

The Grove Gnome is a small creature with a hunched, wiry humanoid frame, about 0.7 m tall. Its body is weathered and natural, colored in leaf greens over bark brown. Its most striking feature is a stone-bearded little figure with gem eyes. It has alert animal eyes. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. It stalks low to the ground, surrounded by drifting pollen and tiny blossoms. Adapted to forest lands, it has moss-green coloring that blends with the undergrowth.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Attacks only if you come close to its home; back away to avoid a fight.

### Vesper-Touched Ifrit `A-`
**Variant (corrupted)** · Dread · Fire (weak: Water) · 5 m · Build: `floating` · Territorial

The Vesper-Touched Ifrit is a huge creature with a bodiless, glowing form, about 5 m tall. Its body is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in ember reds fading to soot black. Look for a torso of fire rising from a smoking lamp. It has eyes set slightly too far apart. An elegant crest and glowing veins of mana mark it as a champion of its kind. It glides without moving its legs, surrounded by heat-shimmer and drifting sparks. Red veins of Vesper light crawl under its skin, its eyes glow crimson, and it grows bolder under the red moon.

- **Field note:** Weak to Water. Attacks only if you come close to its home; back away to avoid a fight.

## Plants & Fungi
*Plant · Fairy-tale Forest · Trope: Screaming mandrakes, walking mushrooms and ancient treants*

### Mandrake `F`
**Core** · Wild · Nature (weak: Fire) · 0.3 m · Build: `plant_walker` · Passive

The Mandrake is a tiny creature with a tall, tree-like walking frame, about 0.3 m tall. Its bark and leaves are weathered and natural, colored in leaf greens over bark brown. Survivors describe a screaming root with a leafy tuft. It has dark, wary eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It stalks low to the ground, surrounded by drifting pollen and tiny blossoms.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Leaves you alone unless attacked.
- **Drops:** Sap, Seed, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Whisperwood, Greymoor, Strife Marches, Sylvaran, Hoshizora +14 more

## Avians
*Avian · High Fantasy · Trope: Griffons, harpies and phoenixes of classic fantasy*

### Giant Crow `F`
**Core** · Wild · Dark (weak: Light) · 0.6 m · Build: `avian` · Territorial

The Giant Crow is a small creature with a broad-winged bird frame, about 0.6 m tall. Its plumage is weathered and natural, colored in ink black with bruised violet. Survivors describe a beak full of stolen shiny things. It has amber predator eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It prowls, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it.

Look for four arms holding four blades. It has burning slit pupils. Faint runes glow along its body when it fights. It circles its prey, surrounded by fine mist and droplets that orbit it. It is far larger than its kin, with a battle-scarred silhouette and a commanding presence; others of its kind follow it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Lightning. Protects something nearby: a nest, a treasure, or a place.

## Centaurs & Taurs
*Beastfolk · Steppe Epic · Trope: Proud nomadic centaur tribes*

### Centaur Scout `C`
**Core** · Wild · Wind (weak: Fire) · 2.4 m · Build: `taur` · Pack

The Centaur Scout is a person-sized creature with a tall half-beast frame, about 2.4 m tall. Its coat is weathered and natural, colored in pale jade and cloud white. Look for a horsebow and a braided mane. It has alert animal eyes. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. It stalks low to the ground, surrounded by swirling leaves and a constant breeze.

## Gremlins
*Fey Pest · Steampunk Mischief · Trope: Machine-breaking pests*

### Gremlin `F`
**Core** · Wild · Lightning (weak: Earth) · 0.6 m · Build: `small_biped` · Swarm

The Gremlin is a small creature with a scrawny biped with long arms and bowed legs, about 0.6 m tall. Its skin is weathered and natural, colored in storm violet shot through with electric yellow. Survivors describe bat ears, a wrench and a laugh like a squeaky hinge. It has alert animal eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It lopes in long strides, surrounded by crackling arcs that jump across its body.

### Thousand-Eyed Watcher `A`
**Core** · Horror · Void (weak: Light) · 6 m · Build: `floating` · Ambush

The Thousand-Eyed Watcher is a huge creature with a bodiless, glowing form, about 6 m tall. Its skin is stretched, stitched and constantly shifting, colored in starless black with faint violet edges. Survivors describe a cloud of eyes that blink in sequence. It has no eyes at all, only a wide and patient mouth. An elegant crest and glowing veins of mana mark it as a champion of its kind. It copies the walk of whatever it hunts, surrounded by warped air, as if space bends around it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.

## Yokai-Inspired Spirits
*Spirit · Japanese Folklore · Trope: Gentle and terrifying spirits (Natsume's Book of Friends, Mushishi, Inuyasha)*

### Umbrella Spirit `F`
**Core** · Charming · Water (weak: Lightning) · 0.8 m · Build: `small_biped` · Passive

The Umbrella Spirit is a small creature with a small, compact humanoid body, about 0.8 m tall. Its form is smooth and clean, colored in deep blues with a glassy shine. Guild sketches always show a one-eyed paper umbrella hopping on one leg. It has wide curious eyes with star-shaped highlights. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It bounces happily, surrounded by fine mist and droplets that orbit it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Lightning. Leaves you alone unless attacked.
- **Drops:** Spirit Paper, Fox Fire, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Hoshizora, Shen-Lu, Haneul, Kitsune Isles, Everdusk
- **Design:** rig `small_biped` + parts [ears_long, bone_trinkets] · material `paper_ink_spirit` · palette #2f7fd1 #9ad7ff #123a5c · VFX `mist_droplets`

### Lantern Ghost `E`
**Core** · Menacing · Fire (weak: Water) · 0.6 m · Build: `floating` · Ambush

The Lantern Ghost is a small creature with a drifting, half-formed body, about 0.6 m tall. Its form is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in ember reds fading to soot black. Look for a paper lantern with a torn mouth. It has eyes that catch torchlight like coins. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It strides with heavy steps, surrounded by heat-shimmer and drifting sparks.

- **Field note:** Weak to Water. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Spirit Paper, Fox Fire, E-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Hoshizora, Shen-Lu, Haneul, Kitsune Isles, Everdusk

## Floor-born
*Dungeon-born · Dungeon Crawl · Trope: Monsters born directly from dungeon walls (DanMachi); dungeon as ecosystem (Dungeon Meshi)*

### Floor Crawler `F`
**Core** · Menacing · None (weak: Fire) · 0.8 m · Build: `quad_small` · Aggressive

The Floor Crawler is a small creature with a compact quadruped frame, about 0.8 m long. Its stone-like hide is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in muted greys and dusty browns. Look for stone-colored skin that matches the walls it was born from. It has eyes that catch torchlight like coins. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It strides with heavy steps. It has no visible aura; you hear its breathing before you see it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Fire. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Dungeon Core Shard, F-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Umbravel, Aurumdeep, The Maw, Great Labyrinth
- **Design:** rig `quad_small` + parts [ears_tall, fur_tufts, horn_single, tail_bushy] · material `dungeon_stone` · palette #8a8f98 #5b5f66 #c9c2b5 · VFX `dust_puffs`

### Wall Stalker `E`
**Core** · Dread · Earth (weak: Wind) · 2 m · Build: `aberrant` · Aggressive

The Wall Stalker is a person-sized creature with a shape that keeps rearranging itself, about 2 m tall. Its stone-like hide is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. It peels itself out of the wall. It has eyes set slightly too far apart. Small scars and rough edges mark it as a survivor. It moves in jerky stop-motion bursts, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Wind. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Dungeon Core Shard, E-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Umbravel, Aurumdeep, The Maw, Great Labyrinth
- **Design:** rig `aberrant` + parts [human_mask, extra_arms] · material `dungeon_stone` · palette #8b5a2b #c2a878 #4a4a3a · VFX `rock_debris`

### Labyrinth Hound `D`
**Core** · Menacing · Earth (weak: Wind) · 1.4 m · Build: `quad_large` · Aggressive

The Labyrinth Hound is a person-sized creature with a heavy, muscular quadruped frame, about 1.4 m long. Its stone-like hide is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Survivors describe a brick-patterned hide. It has burning slit pupils. A distinctive marking runs along its back. It strides with heavy steps, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it.

- **Design:** rig `quad_large` + parts [antlers, mane, armored_back] · material `dungeon_stone` · palette #8b5a2b #c2a878 #4a4a3a · VFX `rock_debris`

### Core Sentinel `C`
**Core** · Menacing · Light (weak: Dark) · 2.5 m · Build: `golem` · Aggressive

The Core Sentinel is a large creature with a heavy, hand-built humanoid shell, about 2.5 m tall. Its stone-like hide is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in warm gold and pearl white. Its most striking feature is a dungeon-core crystal for a heart. It has burning slit pupils. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. It strides with heavy steps, surrounded by soft radiance and floating motes of light.

- **Field note:** Weak to Dark. Attacks on sight. Expect a fight.
- **Drops:** Dungeon Core Shard, C-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Umbravel, Aurumdeep, The Maw, Great Labyrinth

### Maze Weaver `B`
**Core** · Dread · Dark (weak: Light) · 3 m · Build: `octopod` · Guardian

The Maze Weaver is a large creature with a bulbous spider frame on long jointed legs, about 3 m long. Its stone-like hide is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in ink black with bruised violet. It rebuilds corridors behind you. It has eyes that never blink. Faint runes glow along its body when it fights. It moves in jerky stop-motion bursts, surrounded by shadows that lean toward it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Protects something nearby: a nest, a treasure, or a place.
- **Drops:** Dungeon Core Shard, B-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Umbravel, Aurumdeep, The Maw, Great Labyrinth

### Floor Tyrant `A`
**Core** · Menacing · Earth (weak: Wind) · 8 m · Build: `large_biped` · Guardian

The Floor Tyrant is a huge creature with a massive hunched brute frame, about 8 m tall. Its stone-like hide is rough, scarred and spiked, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Its most striking feature is a crown of stalactites. It has eyes that catch torchlight like coins. An elegant crest and glowing veins of mana mark it as a champion of its kind. It charges without hesitation, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it.

- **Field note:** Weak to Wind. Protects something nearby: a nest, a treasure, or a place.

## Vesperites (Moon Raiders)
*Lunar Raider · Cosmic Fantasy · Trope: Serene moon-raiders harvesting gem people (Land of the Lustrous)*

### Vesper Shard-Thrall `C`
**Core** · Dread · Blood (weak: Light) · 1.8 m · Build: `medium_biped` · Ambush

The Vesper Shard-Thrall is a person-sized creature with a lean humanoid frame, about 1.8 m tall. Its porcelain shell is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in arterial crimson and dried-blood brown. Look for porcelain skin cracked with red light. It has eyes that never blink. Battle-worn plates and trophies hang from its body. It freezes whenever it is looked at, surrounded by a faint red mist that smells of iron.

- **Field note:** Weak to Light. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Vesper Glass, Captured Soul-Light, C-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Aldmere, Strife Marches, Sunscar, Lustrine Shores, Vesper
- **Design:** rig `medium_biped` + parts [mask, weapon_socket] · material `porcelain_red_glow` · palette #8b0000 #d7263d #1a0505 · VFX `blood_mist`

## Sandborne

### Sand Skink `F`
**Core** · Wild · Earth (weak: Wind) · 0.5 m · Build: `quad_small` · Ambush

The Sand Skink is a small creature with a low, springy quadruped build, about 0.5 m long. Its sand-scoured hide is weathered and natural, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Look for sand-colored scales and a tongue that tastes for water. It has dark, wary eyes. It has no ornament at all: simple, readable shapes. It lopes in long strides, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it.

### Vesper-Touched Sandworm `A-`
**Variant (corrupted)** · Dread · Earth (weak: Wind) · 40 m · Build: `serpent` · Ambush

The Vesper-Touched Sandworm is a colossal creature with a long, sinuous serpentine body, about 40 m long. Its sand-scoured hide is patchy and subtly wrong, as if assembled from memory, colored in ochre, clay and mossy stone. Its most striking feature is a round mouth ringed with crystal teeth. It has eyes set slightly too far apart. An elegant crest and glowing veins of mana mark it as a champion of its kind. It freezes whenever it is looked at, surrounded by pebbles that tremble and float near it. Red veins of Vesper light crawl under its skin, its eyes glow crimson, and it grows bolder under the red moon.

- **Field note:** Weak to Wind. Waits in hiding. Perception skills and light sources reveal it.
- **Drops:** Desert Glass, Sand Pearl, A-Rank Core
- **Found in:** Sunscar, Zaruma, Dunewhale Drift, Zarvan
- **Design:** rig `serpent` + parts [rattle_tail, fins, scale_ridges, hood] · material `sand_hide` · palette #8b5a2b #c2a878 #4a4a3a · VFX `rock_debris`

### Bandit `F`
